and thus take them as fixed.

For any distribution
F
and
\Psi
define

\displaystyle\varphi_{\Psi}(x)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}x^{T}\Psi^{-1}x\right)

\displaystyle f_{F,\Psi}(x)

\displaystyle=\int\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi)}}\varphi_{\Psi}(x-\tau)dF(\tau),

and for any
F,\alpha,\Omega
define

\displaystyle\psi_{j}(z,\alpha,\Omega,F)

\displaystyle=\log\left(\int\varphi_{\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\hat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)-\tau)dF(\tau)\right).

Denote the posterior mean of
\theta_{j}
under any
F,\alpha,\Omega
by

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{j,F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=a_{j}(\alpha)+B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}\underbrace{E_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}[\tau|\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)]}_{\widehat{\tau}^{*}_{j,F,\alpha,\Omega}},

where
E_{F,\Psi}[h(\tau,Z)|z]
is the posterior mean of
h(\tau,Z)
when
Z=z
under the model
\tau\sim F,Z|\tau\sim N(\tau,\Psi)
:

\displaystyle E_{F,\Psi}[h(\tau,Z)|z]

\displaystyle=\frac{\int h(\tau,z)\varphi_{\Psi}(z-\tau)dF(\tau)}{\int\varphi_{\Psi}(z-\tau)dF(\tau)}.

By Tweedie’s formula

\displaystyle E_{F,\Psi_{j}}[\tau_{j}|\widehat{Z}_{j}]

\displaystyle=\widehat{Z}_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{j,F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=Y_{j}+B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)\frac{\nabla f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}{f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}.

Denote by
\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}
the empirical Bayes posterior mean of
\theta_{j}
and by
\widehat{\theta}_{j,F_{0},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}^{*}\equiv\theta_{j}^{*}
the oracle posterior mean of
\theta_{j}
.
I will collect
\theta_{1},\dots,\theta_{J}
into a
J\times 2
matrix
\theta
. Similarly,
\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{F,\alpha,\Omega}
collects
\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{1,F,\alpha,\Omega},\dots,\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{J,F,\alpha,\Omega}
, and analogously for
\tau
and
\widehat{\tau}^{*}_{F,\alpha,\Omega}
.

For some
\rho>0
define the regularized posterior mean as

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{j,F,\alpha,\Omega,\rho}^{*}=Y_{j}+B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)\frac{\nabla f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega))}{\max(f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det{\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}}})}

and
\theta^{*}_{j,\rho}=\widehat{\theta}_{j,F_{0},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho}^{*}
. Similarly, define

\displaystyle\widehat{\tau}_{j,F,\alpha,\Omega,\rho}^{*}=\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)+\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)\frac{\nabla f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega))}{\max(f_{F,\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det{\widehat{\Psi}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)}}})}

and
\tau^{*}_{j,\rho}=\widehat{\tau}_{j,F_{0},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho}^{*}
.

Define

\displaystyle\varphi_{+}(\rho)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\log\frac{1}{(2\pi\rho)^{2}}},\qquad\rho\in(0,(2\pi)^{-1})

and observe that
\varphi_{+}(\rho)\lesssim\sqrt{\log(1/\rho)}
.

I will choose regularization parameter

\displaystyle\rho_{J}

\displaystyle=\min\left(\frac{1}{J^{4}}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}M_{J}^{2}\Delta_{J}},\frac{1}{2\pi e}\right),

(F.5)

where constant
C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}
will be chosen to satisfy Lemma
F.6
.

F.2
Proof of main result

Define the event

\displaystyle A_{J}

\displaystyle\equiv\left\{\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\bar{Z}_{J}\equiv\max_{j\in[J]}(\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1))\leq M_{J}\right\}

for constants
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
to be chosen. To prove the main result, I consider events
A_{J}
and
A_{J}^{C}
separately. Lemma
F.1
controls MSE regret on
A_{J}^{C}
, while Theorem
F.2
controls MSE regret on
A_{J}
.
While many of the results are true on
A_{J}
for a broad class of
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
, the ones I consider in this proof to obtain the rate of interest are

\displaystyle\Delta_{J}

\displaystyle=C_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{1/2},\qquad M_{J}=(C_{\mathcal{H}}+1)(C_{2,\mathcal{H}}^{-1}\log J)^{1/2},

(F.6)

for constants
C_{\mathcal{H}}
to be chosen and
C_{2,\mathcal{H}}
determined by Theorem SM6.1.

Lemma F.1
.

Under Assumptions
1
,
3
,
8
, and
9
, suppose
\Delta_{J}
and
M_{J}
are of the form (
F.6
) such that
Pr(\bar{Z}_{J}>M_{J})\leq J^{-2}
. Then I can decompose

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}>\Delta_{J})]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}Pr(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}>\Delta_{J})^{1/2}(\log J)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\bar{Z}_{J}>M_{J})]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{J}(\log J).

The proof of Lemma
F.1
is deferred to Supplemental Appendix
F.3
.

Theorem F.2
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
,
3
,
8
, and
9
hold. Fix some
C_{1}>0
, then there exists a constant
C_{\mathcal{H},2}
such that for
\Delta_{J}=C_{1}J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{1/2}
,
M_{J}=C_{\mathcal{H},2}(\log J)^{1/2}
, and corresponding
A_{J}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E\left[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1}(\log J)^{6}.

The proof of Theorem
F.2
is deferred to Supplemental Appendix
F.4
.

Combining these two results, I obtain the result of Theorem
E.4
:

Proof of Theorem
E.4
.

Let
\Delta_{J}=C_{1,\mathcal{H}}J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{1/2}
and
M_{J}=C\sqrt{\log J}
, where
C_{1,\mathcal{H}}
is the constant in Assumption
8
(4) and
C
is chosen in application of Theorem
F.2
. Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}>\Delta_{J})]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\bar{Z}_{J}>M_{J})]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1}(\log J)^{6}+J^{-1}(\log J)

Theorem
F.2
, Lemma
F.1
, Assumption
8
(4)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1}(\log J)^{6}.

∎

F.3
Proof of Lemma
F.1

Proof of Lemma
F.1
.

Observe that for any event
A
on the data
Z_{1:J}
, by Cauchy-Schwarz

\displaystyle E\left[\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(A)\right]

\displaystyle\leq E\left[\left(\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}\right]^{1/2}Pr(A)^{1/2}.

Apply Lemma
F.3
to get

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\bar{Z}_{J}^{4},

since
\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
under Assumption
8
.

Apply Lemma
F.4
to get
E[\bar{Z}_{J}^{4}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{2}
. This proves both claims.
∎

Lemma F.3
.

Assume that
\widehat{F}_{J}
is supported within
[-C\bar{M}_{J},C\bar{M}_{J}]^{2}
for some constant
C
where
\bar{M}_{J}=\max_{j}(\max(\|\widehat{Z}_{j}(\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega})\|_{2},1))
. Suppose
\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
, and Assumptions
1
,
3
, and
8
hold. Then letting
\bar{Z}_{J}=\max(\max_{j}\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\bar{Z}_{J}.

Proof.

By Tweedie’s formula,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\theta}_{j,\widehat{F},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}-\theta_{j}^{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}))}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}))}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}))}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}))}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}\left[\tau_{j}-\widehat{Z}_{j}|\widehat{Z}_{j}\right]-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}E_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}\left[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|Z_{j}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\bar{M}_{J}+\bar{Z}_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\max_{j}\max(\|\widehat{Z}_{j}\|_{2},\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)

by the boundedness of
\widehat{F}_{J}
and by Lemma
F.18
.
Note that
\|\widehat{Z}_{j}\|_{2}=\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}Z_{j}+B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\left(a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})\right)\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\bar{Z}_{J}
. The result follows.
∎

Lemma F.4
.

Let
\bar{Z}_{J}=\max(\max_{j}\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)
. Under Assumptions
1
and
8
, for
t>1

\displaystyle Pr(\bar{Z}_{J}>t)\leq 2J\exp(-C_{\mathcal{H}}t^{2})\quad\text{and}\quad E[\bar{Z}_{J}^{p}]\lesssim_{p,\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{p/2}.

Moreover, if
M_{J}=(C_{\mathcal{H}}+1)(C_{2,\mathcal{H}}^{-1}\log J)^{1/2}
then for all sufficiently large choices of
C_{\mathcal{H}}
,
Pr(\bar{Z}_{J}>M_{J})\leq J^{-2}
.

Proof.

The first claim is immediate under a union bound and noting that each
\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}
is a subexponential random variable, so
Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}>t)\leq 2\exp(-C_{\mathcal{H}}t)\Rightarrow Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>t)\leq 2\exp(-C_{\mathcal{H}}t^{2})
.

The second claim follows from the observation that

\displaystyle E[\max_{j}(\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1))^{p}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[(\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1))^{pc}]\right)^{1/c}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}E[(\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{2},1))^{pc/2}]\right)^{1/c}

\displaystyle\leq J^{1/c}C_{\mathcal{H}}^{p}(pc)^{p/2}.

where the last inequality follows from
\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}
being a subexponential random variable. Choose
c=\log J
for
J^{1/\log J}=e
to finish the proof. The moreover part follows exactly as in the proof of Lemma OA3.7 in
Chen (2026)
.
∎

F.4
Proof of Theorem
F.2

Proof of Theorem
F.2
.

Choose
M_{J}
to be of the form (
F.6
). By triangle inequality

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}^{*}\|_{F}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}^{*}-\theta^{*}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}\|_{F}+\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\theta_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}+\|\theta_{\rho_{J}}^{*}-\theta^{*}\|_{F}.

Define

\displaystyle\xi_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\xi_{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}^{*}-\theta^{*}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\xi_{3}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\theta_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\xi_{4}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\theta_{\rho_{J}}^{*}-\theta^{*}\|_{F}^{2}.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}E\left[\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}^{*}-\theta^{*}\|_{F}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})\right]\leq 4\left(E\xi_{1}+E\xi_{2}+E\xi_{3}+E\xi_{4}\right).

By Lemma
F.5
,
\xi_{1}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{2}\Delta_{J}^{2}
and thus
E\xi_{1}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{2}\Delta_{J}^{2}
.

By Lemma
F.6
, the truncation is not binding for the choice of
\rho_{J}
in the lemma, so
\xi_{2}=0
.

By Lemma
F.9
,
E\xi_{3}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}
for
\delta_{J}=J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{3/2}
, as in Corollary
F.8
.

By Lemma
F.7
,
E\xi_{4}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{J}
.

Thus the
E[\xi_{3}]
rate is the dominating rate and the result follows from plugging in for
\delta_{J}
.
∎

Lemma F.5
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
F.2
,
\xi_{1}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{2}\Delta_{J}^{2}
.

Proof.

Using Taylor’s theorem and the equivalence of norms in fixed dimensions I can write

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}-\theta^{*}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Sigma_{j}\right\|_{op}\left\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{-1/2}\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\Sigma_{j}\right\|_{op}\left\|\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}-\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sup_{(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{C})\in\mathcal{L}_{j}}\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{C}^{2}}\right\|_{F}\left\|a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})-a_{j}(\alpha_{0})\right\|_{\infty}+\sup_{(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{C})\in\mathcal{L}_{j}}\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{C}^{2}}\right\|_{F}\left\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\right\|_{op},

where
\mathcal{L}_{j}
denotes the line segment between
(a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha}),B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2})
and
(a_{j}(\alpha_{0}),B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2})
. Using the bounds on derivatives obtained in Lemma
F.17
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})\left\|\widehat{\theta}^{*}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega}}-\theta^{*}_{j,\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}(\log J)\Delta_{J}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\xi_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{2}\Delta_{J}^{2}.

∎

Lemma F.6
.

Suppose
\bar{Z}_{J}=\max_{j\in[J]}\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)\leq M_{J},\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J}
. Let
\widehat{F}_{J}
satisfy Assumption
9
and
\widehat{\chi}
satisfy Assumption
8
. Then for
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
of the form (
F.6
),

1.

\max(\|\widehat{Z}_{j}\|_{2},1)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}

2.

There exists
C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}
such that with
\rho_{J}=\min\left(\frac{1}{J^{4}}\exp(-C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}M_{J}^{2}\Delta_{J}),\frac{1}{2\pi e}\right)
,
f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})\geq\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}
.

3.

The above
\rho_{J}
satisfies
\log(1/\rho_{J})\asymp_{\mathcal{H}}\log J,\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})\asymp\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}\asymp_{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\log J}
, and
\rho_{J}\lesssim J^{-4}
.

Proof.

For claim (1), recall that

\displaystyle\widehat{Z}_{j}

\displaystyle=B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}Z_{j}+B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\left(a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})\right)

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\max(\|\widehat{Z}_{j}\|_{2},1)

\displaystyle\leq\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}Z_{j}\|_{2}+\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\left(a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})\right)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\|_{op}\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\|_{op}\|Z_{j}\|_{2}+\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}\|_{op}\|a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}+\Delta_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}.

For claim (2), I can follow the proof of Theorem 5 in
Jiang (2020)
but for a multivariate distribution for a random vector in
\mathbb{R}^{2}
:
Let
\widehat{F}_{J,j}=(1-\varepsilon)\widehat{F}_{J}+\varepsilon\delta_{\widehat{Z}_{j}}
. Then
f_{\widehat{F}_{J,j},\widehat{\Psi}_{i}}(\widehat{Z}_{i})\geq(1-\varepsilon)f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{i}}(\widehat{Z}_{i})
and
f_{\widehat{F}_{J,j},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})\geq\frac{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\widehat{\Psi}_{j})}}
. So by Assumption
9
,

\displaystyle\prod_{i=1}^{J}f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{i}}(\widehat{Z}_{i})

\displaystyle\geq\exp(-J\kappa_{J})\prod_{i=1}^{J}f_{\widehat{F}_{J,j},\widehat{\Psi}_{i}}(\widehat{Z}_{i})\geq\exp(-J\kappa_{J})(1-\varepsilon)^{J-1}\frac{\varepsilon}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\widehat{\Psi}_{j})}}\prod_{i\neq j}f_{\widehat{F}_{J,i},\widehat{\Psi}_{i}}(\widehat{Z}_{i}).

Thus taking
\varepsilon=1/J
and canceling terms,
f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})\geq\frac{\exp(-J\kappa_{J})}{2\pi eJ\sqrt{\det(\widehat{\Psi}_{j})}}
. Plugging in
\kappa_{J}=\frac{3}{J}\log\left(\frac{J}{(2\pi e)^{1/3}}\right)
gives

\displaystyle f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\widehat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{J^{4}\sqrt{\det(\widehat{\Psi}_{j})}},

that is,

\displaystyle\int\frac{1}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)^{T}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\right)d\widehat{F}_{J}(\tau)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{J^{4}}.

Note that

\displaystyle(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)^{T}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)

\displaystyle=\left(\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\right)^{T}\left(\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\right)

and one can verify that

\displaystyle\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)+\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}(a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha}))+\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}(B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2})\tau.

Let

\displaystyle\xi(\tau)

\displaystyle\equiv\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}(a_{j}(\alpha_{0})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha}))+\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}(B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2})\tau

and note that
\|\xi(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}M_{J}
over the support of
\tau
under
\widehat{F}_{J}
.
Then

\displaystyle\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)^{T}\widehat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)+\xi(\tau)\right)^{T}\left(\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)+\xi(\tau)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\Psi_{j}^{-1}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\xi(\tau)^{T}\xi(\tau)-\xi(\tau)^{T}\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\Psi_{j}^{-1}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\exp\left(C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}\Delta_{J}M_{J}\left\|\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right\|_{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\Psi_{j}^{-1}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right)\exp\left(C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}\Delta_{J}M_{J}^{2}\right)

where
C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}
is defined by optimizing the quadratic expression over
\|\xi(\tau)\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}M_{J}
and the final line follows because
\left\|\Sigma_{j}^{-1/2}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}
.
Thus

\displaystyle\int\frac{1}{2\pi}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\Psi_{j}^{-1}(Z_{j}-\tau)\right)d\widehat{F}_{J}(\tau)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{J^{4}}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}\Delta_{J}M_{J}^{2}}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\frac{1}{J^{4}}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}\Delta_{J}M_{J}^{2}}.

For claim (3), I calculate
\log(1/\rho_{J})=\max(4\log J+C_{\mathcal{H},\rho}M_{J}^{2}\Delta_{J},\log(2\pi e))\asymp_{\mathcal{H}}\log J
, noting
M_{J}^{2}\Delta_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{3/2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
.
∎

Lemma F.7
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
F.2
, in the proof of Theorem
F.2

E\xi_{4}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{J}
.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle E\left[\|\theta^{*}_{j,\rho_{J}}-\theta^{*}_{j}\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=E\left[\left\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det{\Psi_{j}}}})}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})\|_{op}E\left[\left\|\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\right\|_{2}^{4}\right]^{1/2}E\left[\left(1-\frac{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det{\Psi_{j}}}})}\right)^{4}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})\|_{op}E\left[\|E_{F_{0},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}}\left[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|Z_{j}\right]\|_{2}^{4}\right]^{1/2}E\left[\left(1-\frac{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det{\Psi_{j}}}})}\right)^{4}\right]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})\|_{op}E\left[\|\tau_{j}-Z_{j}\|_{2}^{4}\right]^{1/2}Pr\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})<\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det{\Psi_{j}}}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})\|_{op}E\left[\|\tau_{j}-Z_{j}\|_{2}^{4}\right]^{1/2}\rho_{J}^{1/4}\left(Var(Z_{j}^{w})+Var(Z_{j}^{g})\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\rho_{J}^{1/4}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{J},

where the second line follows from submultiplicativity and Cauchy-Schwarz, the third line from Tweedie’s formula, the fourth line from Jensen’s inequality, the fifth line from Lemma
F.15
, and the final line from Lemma
F.6
.
∎

Corollary F.8
.

Assume Assumptions
1
,
3
,
8
, and
9
hold. Suppose
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
take the form (
F.6
). Define the rate function

\displaystyle\delta_{J}=J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{3/2}.

Then there exists some constant
B_{\mathcal{H}}
, depending solely on
C_{\mathcal{H}}^{*}
in Corollary
F.12
and
\mathcal{H}
such that

\displaystyle Pr\left(A_{J},\bar{h}(f_{\hat{F}_{J},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})>B_{\mathcal{H}}\delta_{J}\right)\leq\left(\frac{\log\log J}{\log 2}+10\right)\frac{1}{J}.

The proof of Corollary
F.8
is deferred to Supplemental Appendix
F.5
.

Lemma F.9
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
F.2
, in the proof of Theorem
F.2
,
E\xi_{3}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}
, for
\delta_{J}=J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{3/2}
, as in Corollary
F.8
.

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\theta}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\theta_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}(\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*})\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\|_{op}\|\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}.

Thus to control
\xi_{3}
I will control the object
\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}
.

Let
B_{J}=\{\bar{h}(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})<B_{\mathcal{H}}\delta_{J}\}
for constant
B_{\mathcal{H}}
in Corollary
F.8
.
Let
F_{1},\dots,F_{N}
be a set of prior distributions that is a minimal
\omega
-covering of
\{F:\bar{h}(f_{F,\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\leq B_{\mathcal{H}}\delta_{J}\}
in the metric

\displaystyle d_{M_{J},\rho_{J}}(H_{1},H_{2})=\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{z:\|z\|_{2}\leq M_{J}}\left\|\frac{\Psi_{j}\nabla f_{H_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{H_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}-\frac{\Psi_{j}\nabla f_{H_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{H_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\right\|_{2},

where
N\leq N(\omega/2,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{M_{J},\rho_{J}})
by monotonicity relation of covering numbers, as in
Chen (2026)
.
Let
\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}
be the posterior mean vector corresponding to prior
F_{i}
with conditional moments
\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0}
and regularization parameter
\rho_{J}
.
Then

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})}{J}\|\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4}{J}\left(\zeta_{1}^{2}+\zeta_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{3}^{2}+\zeta_{4}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\|\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J}\cap B_{J}^{C})

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\left(\|\widehat{\tau}_{\widehat{F}_{J},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\rho_{J}}^{*}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}-\max_{i\in[N]}\|\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}\right)^{2}_{+}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J}\cap B_{J})

\displaystyle\zeta_{3}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{i\in[N]}\left(\|\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}-E[\|\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}]\right)^{2}_{+}

\displaystyle\zeta_{4}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{i\in[N]}\left(E[\|\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\|_{F}]\right)^{2}.

I will show that
\frac{1}{J}E[\zeta_{1}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{\log J\log\log J}{J}
,
\frac{1}{J}E[\zeta_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{3}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{(\log J)^{4}}{J}
, and
\frac{1}{J}E[\zeta_{4}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}
.
By definition of
\delta_{J}
, the dominating rate is
(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{(\log J)^{6}}{J}
.
Thus
E[\xi_{3}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}
.

To control
\zeta_{1}
:
From section D.3.1 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}E\zeta_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})^{2}Pr(A_{J}\cap B_{J}^{C})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)Pr(A_{J}\cap B_{J}^{C}).

By Corollary
F.8
,
Pr(A_{J}\cap B_{J}^{C})\leq\left(\frac{\log\log J}{\log 2}+10\right)\frac{1}{J}
and hence
\frac{1}{J}E\zeta_{1}^{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{\log J\log\log J}{J}
.

To control
\zeta_{2}
and
\zeta_{3}
:
As in section OA3.2.2 in
Chen (2026)
and section D.3.2 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, on
A_{J}\cap B_{J}
I can write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{J}\zeta_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}(A_{J}\cap B_{J})\min_{i\in[N]}\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}\leq M_{J})\left\|\frac{\Psi_{j}\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}})}-\frac{\Psi_{j}\nabla f_{F_{i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max(f_{F_{i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}})}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\omega^{2}.

Section D.3.3 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
gives us

\displaystyle E\zeta_{3}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J}))^{2}\log(eN)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J\log N

using Lemma
F.6
.
Following section D.3.5 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, I will choose
\omega=2\left(\bar{k}^{3/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})+\bar{k}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{J}
.
Note that by section D.3.5 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
and because
J\geq\frac{5}{\underline{k}}
, I can bound the metric entropy

\displaystyle\log N\left(\left(\bar{k}^{3/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})+\bar{k}^{2}\right)\frac{1}{J},\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{M_{J},\rho_{J}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{2}M_{J}^{2}.

Also
\frac{1}{J}\zeta_{2}^{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}J^{-1}\sqrt{\log J}
.
Thus
\frac{1}{J}E[\zeta_{2}^{2}+\zeta_{3}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{(\log J)^{3}M_{J}^{2}}{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{(\log J)^{4}}{J}
.

To control
\zeta_{4}
:
As in Section D.3.4 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, using Lemma E.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
I can write

\displaystyle\left(E\left\|\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{(i)}-\tau_{\rho_{J}}^{*}\right\|_{F}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\max\left\{(\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J}))^{6},\left|\log h\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{F^{(i)},\Psi_{j}}\right)\right|\right\}h^{2}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{F^{(i)},\Psi_{j}}\right).

Then following the exact same argument in section OA3.2.4 of
Chen (2026)
,
\frac{1}{J}E\zeta_{4}^{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{3}\delta_{J}^{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{6}J^{-1}
.
∎

F.5
Proof of Corollary
F.8

F.5.1
Derivative computations

I first compute derivatives of
\psi_{j}
with respect to
a_{j}(\alpha)
and
B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}
, which will be useful in later proofs.
Let
\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi}(z)
denote the Hessian matrix of
f_{F,\Psi}
evaluated at
z
. Since all derivatives are evaluated at
F,\alpha,\Omega
, I denote
\hat{z}=\widehat{Z}_{j}(\alpha,\Omega)
and
\hat{\Psi}_{j}=B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}\Sigma_{j}B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}
.
Then

\displaystyle\frac{\nabla f_{F,\Psi}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi}(z)}

\displaystyle=\Psi^{-1}E[\tau-Z|z]

\displaystyle\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi}(z)}

\displaystyle=\Psi^{-1}E[(\tau-Z)(\tau-Z)^{T}|z]\Psi^{-1}-\Psi^{-1}

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=-B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}\frac{\nabla f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}{f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}E[Z_{j}-\tau_{j}|\hat{z}]

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}\left(\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}{f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}-\frac{\nabla f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})\nabla f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})^{T}}{f^{2}_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}\right)B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}\bigg(\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}E[(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})^{T}|\hat{z}]\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt-\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}-\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}E[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|\hat{z}]E[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|\hat{z}]^{T}\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}\bigg)B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial B_{j}^{1/2}}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\hat{\Psi}_{j})}f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}\int\varphi_{\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\tau^{T}dF(\tau)

\displaystyle=\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}E[(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}|\hat{z}]

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2})}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\hat{\Psi}_{j})}f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}\underbrace{\int\varphi_{\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\text{vec}\left((\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\tau^{T}\right)dF(\tau)}_{Q_{j}(\widehat{Z}_{j},F,\hat{\Psi}_{j})}

\displaystyle=(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2})E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)|\hat{z}\right]

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\hat{\Psi}_{j})}f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}\int\varphi_{\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\bigg\{\text{vec}\left((\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)\tau^{T}\right)(\widehat{Z}_{j}-\tau)^{T}\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 200.0pt-\tau\otimes I_{2}\bigg\}B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}dF(\tau)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2})\frac{Q_{j}(Z_{j},F,\hat{\Psi}_{j})}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\hat{\Psi}_{j})}f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}\frac{(\nabla f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z}))^{T}}{f_{F,\hat{\Psi}_{j}}(\hat{z})}B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle=(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2})E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau)\tau^{T}\right)(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}-\tau\otimes I_{2}|\hat{z}\right]B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2})E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)|\hat{z}\right]E\left[(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})^{T}|\hat{z}\right]\hat{\Psi}_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega)^{-1/2}

While calculation of the derivative
\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{F,\alpha,\Omega}
is tricky, one can verify that it is the weighted sum of posterior means

E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)^{T}|\hat{z}\right]
,

E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)|\hat{z}\right]E\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)|\hat{z}\right]^{T}
,

E[Z\tau^{T}|\hat{z}],

and
E[\text{vec}(Z-\tau)\tau^{T}|\hat{z}]
, with weights that are simple functions of
\Sigma_{j}
and
B_{j}(\Omega)
.

F.5.2
Preliminary results

Throughout this subsection I use the following high-level assumption on rates
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
, which is exactly Assumption SM6.1 in
Chen (2026)
. Note that the assumption is satisfied for the choice (
F.6
).

Assumption F.10
.

Assume that 1)
\frac{1}{\sqrt{J}}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{-3}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
, and 2)
\sqrt{\log J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}
.

Much of this subsection will be focused on proving the following result.

Theorem F.11
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
F.2
and Assumption
F.10
, there exist constants
C_{1,\mathcal{H}},C_{2,\mathcal{H}}>0
such that the following tail bound holds: Let

\displaystyle\epsilon_{J}

\displaystyle=M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}\Delta_{J}\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}h\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}\right)+\Delta_{J}\log Je^{-C_{2,\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}+\Delta_{J}^{2}M_{J}^{2}\log J+\frac{M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{3/2}\Delta_{J}}{\sqrt{J}}.

Then

\displaystyle Pr\left(\bar{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J},\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\text{Sub}_{J}(\widehat{F}_{J})>C_{1,\mathcal{H}}\epsilon_{J}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{J}.

Plugging in the rates (
F.6
), I obtain the following corollary:

Corollary F.12
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
F.2
, suppose
\Delta_{J},M_{J}
are of the form (
F.6
). Then there exists a constant
C_{\mathcal{H}}^{*}
such that the following tail bound holds: Let

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{J}

\displaystyle=J^{-1/2}(\log J)^{3/2}\bar{h}\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot}\right)+J^{-1}(\log J)^{3},

then

\displaystyle Pr\left(A_{J},\text{Sub}_{J}(\widehat{F}_{J})>C_{H}^{*}\varepsilon_{J}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{J}.

The proof follows exactly as the proof of Corollary SM6.1 of
Chen (2026)
but plugging in the rates for
\Delta_{J}
and
M_{J}
from (
F.6
).

Proof of Theorem
F.11
.

As in section SM6.2.1 of
Chen (2026)
, if I construct random variables
a_{J}
and
b_{J}
such that on the event
A_{J}
,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega},\widehat{F}_{J})-\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\widehat{F}_{J})\right|

\displaystyle\leq a_{J},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega},F_{0})-\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},F_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq b_{J},

then to prove the theorem it suffices to show that
Pr(\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})(a_{J}+b_{J}+\kappa_{J})\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\epsilon_{J})\leq\frac{9}{J}
.

Let
\Delta_{\alpha,j}=a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})-a_{j}(\alpha_{0})
,
\Delta_{\Omega,j}=\text{vec}(B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2})-\text{vec}(B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2})
, and
\Delta_{j}\equiv(\Delta_{\alpha,j}^{T},\Delta_{\Omega,j}^{T})^{T}
. I can take a second-order Taylor expansion of
\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega},\widehat{F}_{J})-\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\widehat{F}_{J})
around
(a_{j}(\alpha),\text{vec}(B_{j}(\Omega)^{1/2}))
:

\displaystyle\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega},\widehat{F}_{J})-\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},\widehat{F}_{J})

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0}}\Delta_{\alpha,j}+\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0}}\Delta_{\Omega,j}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\Delta_{j}^{T}H_{j}(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega},\widehat{F}_{J})\Delta_{j}}_{R_{1j}},

where
H_{j}(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega},\widehat{F}_{J})
is the Hessian matrix with respect to
(a_{j}(\alpha),\text{vec}(B_{j}(\Omega))^{1/2})
.

Truncate the denominators of the first derivatives by Lemma
F.6
for the choice of
\rho_{J}
in (
F.5
), which does not bind, so that

\displaystyle D_{\alpha,j}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})

\displaystyle\equiv-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{-1/2}\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}=\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0}}

\displaystyle D_{\Omega,j}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{(I_{2}\otimes\Sigma_{j}^{-1}B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2})Q_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j})}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j}),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}=\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0}}.

Defining

\displaystyle\overline{D}_{k,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})

\displaystyle=\int D_{k,j}(z,\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz\qquad\text{for }k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\},

as in section SM6.2.2 of
Chen (2026)
I define for each
k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}

\displaystyle U_{1k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\overline{D}_{k,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})^{T}\Delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle U_{2k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left[D_{k,j}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-\overline{D}_{k,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right]^{T}\Delta_{k,j}

\displaystyle R_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}R_{1j}

and let

\displaystyle a_{J}

\displaystyle=|R_{1}|+\sum_{k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}}|U_{1k}|+|U_{2k}|.

Similarly take a Taylor expansion

\displaystyle\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\widehat{\alpha},\widehat{\Omega},F_{0})-\psi_{j}(Z_{j},\alpha_{0},\Omega_{0},F_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{F_{0},\chi_{0}}\Delta_{\alpha,j}+\frac{\partial\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{F_{0},\chi_{0}}\Delta_{\Omega,j}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\Delta_{j}^{T}H_{j}(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega},F_{0})\Delta_{j}}_{R_{2j}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}}D_{k,j}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)^{T}\Delta_{k,j}+R_{2j}

\displaystyle\equiv U_{3\alpha j}+U_{3\Omega j}+R_{2j}.

Defining
U_{3k}=\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}U_{3kj}
for
k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}
and
R_{2}=\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}R_{2j}
, let

\displaystyle b_{J}

\displaystyle=|R_{2}|+\sum_{k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}}|U_{3k}|.

Note that

\displaystyle a_{J}+b_{J}+\kappa_{J}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{J}+|R_{1}|+|R_{2}|+\sum_{k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}}|U_{1k}|+|U_{2k}|+|U_{3k}|.

I now bound each term individually, following
Chen (2026)
.

Bounding
U_{1\alpha}
:

I will follow the proof of Lemma SM6.1 in
Chen (2026)
to show that

\displaystyle|U_{1\alpha}|

\displaystyle\equiv\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\overline{D}_{\alpha,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})^{T}\Delta_{\alpha,j}\right|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\left[\frac{\sqrt{\log J}}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}h\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)+\frac{M_{J}^{1/2}}{J}\right].

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{D}_{\alpha,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\left\|\int\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\int\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\left[f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right]dz\right\|_{2}

(F.7)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\int\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz\right\|_{2}.

(F.8)

Following section SM6.3.1 of
Chen (2026)
,

\displaystyle[\eqref{eq:sm6.29}]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim h^{2}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)\int\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left(\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)\right)^{2}}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)+f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right)dz.

By Lemmas
F.6
and
F.14
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left(\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho_{J})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\eqref{eq:sm6.29}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}h\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)\sqrt{\log J}.

As in section SM6.3.2 of
Chen (2026)
, by Cauchy-Schwarz

(
F.8
)

\displaystyle\leq\int\left\|\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\right\|_{2}\mathbbm{1}\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)\leq\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{E_{Z\sim f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\left[\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\left[\tau-Z|Z\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]}\sqrt{Pr_{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z)\leq\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}.

By Jensen’s inequality and law of iterated expectations, the first term is

\displaystyle\sqrt{E_{Z\sim f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\left[\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\left[\tau-Z|Z\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\sqrt{E_{\tau\sim\widehat{F}_{J},Z\sim N(\tau,\Psi_{j})}\left[\left\|\tau-Z\right\|_{2}^{2}|Z\right]}

\displaystyle=\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\sqrt{\text{tr}(\Psi_{j})}.

By Lemma
F.15
, the second term is bounded by a constant times
\rho_{J}^{1/4}\left(\text{tr}\left(Var_{Z\sim f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}(Z)\right)\right)^{1/4}
and
\text{tr}\left(Var_{Z\sim f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}(Z)\right)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}
, so by Lemma
F.6
,
\eqref{eq:sm6.30}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\rho_{J}^{1/4}M_{J}^{1/2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{1/2}J^{-1}
.
The result follows by using these results to bound
|U_{1\alpha}|
.

Bounding
U_{1\Omega}
:

I will follow the proof of Lemma SM6.2 in
Chen (2026)
to show that

\displaystyle|U_{1\Omega}|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\left[\frac{M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}h\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}\right)+\frac{M_{J}^{3/2}}{J}\right].

As in the proof to bound
U_{1\alpha}
, decompose

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{D}_{\Omega,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\left\|\int\frac{Q_{j}(z,\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j})}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\left[f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right]dz\right\|_{2}

(F.9)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\int\frac{Q_{j}(z,\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j})}{\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz\right\|_{2}.

(F.10)

Following section SM6.4.1 of
Chen (2026)
, from Lemma
F.16

\displaystyle[\eqref{eq:sm6.33}]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim h^{2}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)\int\frac{\left\|Q_{j}(z,\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j})\right\|_{2}^{2}}{\left(\max\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}},\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)\right)^{2}}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)+f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right)dz

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}h^{2}\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)\log J

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\eqref{eq:sm6.33}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}h\left(f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}},f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\right)\sqrt{\log J}.

As in section SM6.4.2 of
Chen (2026)
, by Cauchy-Schwarz

(
F.10
)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{E_{Z\sim f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\left[\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}\left[(Z-\tau)\tau^{T}|Z\right]\right\|_{F}^{2}\right]}\sqrt{Pr_{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}}\left(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\Psi_{j}}(Z)\leq\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\rho_{J}^{1/4}M_{J}^{1/2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{3/2}J^{-1}.

Bounding
U_{2\alpha},U_{2\Omega}
:

I will follow the proof of Lemma SM6.3 in
Chen (2026)
to show that for
k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}
,

\displaystyle Pr\left(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J},|U_{2k}|\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}r_{J}\right)\leq\frac{2}{J}

for
r_{J}=\Delta_{J}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}\log J+\frac{M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{J}}\Delta_{J}
.

I will choose some
\overline{U}_{2k}
such that if
\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J}
and
\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J}
then
|U_{2k}|\leq\overline{U}_{2k}
. Thus a bound on
Pr(\overline{U}_{2k}>t)
suffices.

Define

\displaystyle D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})

\displaystyle=D_{k,j}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}\leq M_{J})

\displaystyle\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})

\displaystyle=\int D_{k,j,M_{J}}(z,\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz.

On
\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J}
note that

\displaystyle|U_{2k}|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\{D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\}^{T}\Delta_{k,j}\right|

(F.11)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\{\overline{D}_{k,j}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(\widehat{F}_{J},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\}^{T}\Delta_{k,j}\right|.

(F.12)

By Lemmas
F.16
and
F.6
, uniformly over all
F
,

\displaystyle\left\|D_{k,j}(z,F,\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\|z\|_{2}\sqrt{\log J}+\log J.

(F.13)

Thus

(
F.12
)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\left(\sqrt{\log J}\max_{j\in[J]}\int_{\|z\|_{2}>M_{J}}\|z\|_{2}f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz+\log J\max_{j\in[J]}Pr_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})\right).

By Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\int_{\|z\|_{2}>M_{J}}\|z\|_{2}f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{E[\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{2}]Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})}.

Because each
Z_{j}
is such that
Z_{j}|\tau_{j}\sim N(\tau_{j},\Psi_{j})
and
\tau_{j}\sim F_{0}
which is mean zero, each
Z_{j}
is sub-Gaussian so that
Pr_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})\leq\exp(-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2})
for some constant
C_{\mathcal{H}}
.
Thus
\eqref{eq:sm6.37}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}\log J
.

To bound (
F.11
), let
F_{1},\dots,F_{N}
be a minimal
\omega
-covering of distributions on
\mathbb{R}^{2}
,
\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
, under the pseudometric

\displaystyle d_{k,\infty,M_{J}}(F_{1},F_{2})

\displaystyle=\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{\|z\|_{2}\leq M_{J}}\left\|D_{k,j}(z,F_{1},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-D_{k,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\|_{2},

(F.14)

taking
N=N(\omega,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{k,\infty,M_{J}})
. Project
\widehat{F}_{J}
to the
\omega
-covering to obtain

(
F.11
)

\displaystyle\leq 2\omega\Delta_{J}+\max_{i\in[N]}\left|\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\{D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},F_{i},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{i},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\}^{T}\Delta_{k,j}\right|.

Define

\displaystyle v_{i,j}(\chi)

\displaystyle\equiv\left\{D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},F_{i},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{i},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\}^{T}\Delta_{k,j}(\chi),\qquad V_{J,i}(\chi)\equiv\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}v_{i,j}(\chi)

for
\Delta_{\alpha,j}(\tilde{\chi})=a_{j}(\tilde{\alpha})-a_{j}(\alpha_{0})
and
\Delta_{\Omega,j}(\tilde{\chi})=\text{vec}(B_{j}(\tilde{\Omega})^{1/2})-\text{vec}(B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2})
.
Then it follows that
\eqref{eq:sm6.36}\lesssim\omega\Delta_{J}+\max_{i\in[N]}\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J,i}(\chi)|
where

\displaystyle S

\displaystyle=\left\{\chi=(\alpha,\Omega):\|\chi-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\left(a(\cdot;\alpha),B(\cdot,\Omega)^{1/2}\right)\in\mathcal{C}\right\}

(F.15)

for
\mathcal{C}
as defined in Assumption
8
.
Thus for some
\omega
to be chosen, let

\displaystyle\overline{U}_{2k}=C_{\mathcal{H}}\left\{\Delta_{J}(\log J)e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}+\omega\Delta_{J}+\max_{i\in[N]}\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J,i}(\chi)|\right\}.

To bound
Pr(\overline{U}_{2k}>t)
I first look at the empirical process
\max_{i\in[N]}\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J,i}(\chi)|
.
For fixed
\chi,\chi_{1},\chi_{2}\in S
, because the
v_{i,j}(\chi)
are independent across
j
and bounded, and because

\displaystyle\left\|D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},F_{i},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\right\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}

as shown in (
F.13
), it follows that

\displaystyle\left\|V_{J,i}(\chi)\right\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}}{\sqrt{J}}\Delta_{J},

\displaystyle\left\|V_{J,i}(\chi_{1})-V_{J,i}(\chi_{2})\right\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}}{\sqrt{J}}\left\|\chi_{1}-\chi_{2}\right\|_{J}.

Thus
\chi\mapsto V_{J,i(\chi)}
is a process with sub-Gaussian increments under
\|\cdot\|_{J}
. By the same chaining argument as in the proof of Lemma SM6.3 of
Chen (2026)
, for every
u>0
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J,i}(\chi)|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}}{\sqrt{J}}\left[(1+u)\Delta_{J}+\int_{0}^{\infty}\sqrt{\log N(\varepsilon,S,\|\cdot\|_{J})}d\varepsilon\right]

with probability at least
1-2e^{-u^{2}}
.

I know
N(\varepsilon,S,\|\cdot\|_{J})\leq N(\varepsilon,\mathcal{C},\|\cdot\|_{\infty})
and
S
has diameter at most
2\Delta_{J}
under
\|\cdot\|_{J}
. Thus

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\sqrt{\log N(\varepsilon,S,\|\cdot\|_{J})}d\varepsilon

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\int_{0}^{2\Delta_{J}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{C}}}{\varepsilon}\right)}d\varepsilon\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{C}}}{\Delta_{J}}\right)}.

So by union bound,

\displaystyle Pr\left(\max_{i\in[N]}\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J,i}(\chi)|\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}}{\sqrt{J}}\left[(1+u)\Delta_{J}+\Delta_{J}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{C}}}{\Delta_{J}}\right)}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2Ne^{-u^{2}},

choosing
u=\sqrt{\log N}+\sqrt{\log J}
so that the right hand side is bounded by
2/J
.
Taking
\omega=M_{J}\frac{1+\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{J}}\geq\frac{1+\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{J}}
, by Lemma
F.19
, Lemma
F.6
, and Assumption
F.10
,

\displaystyle\log N(\omega,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\alpha,\infty,M_{J}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{2}M_{J}^{2}

\displaystyle\log N(\omega,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\Omega,\infty,M_{J}})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log J)^{2}M_{J}^{2}.

Note that this means
\omega\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J}}(\log J)^{1/2}M_{J}

and
(1+u)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\log J
. In order to do this I require
\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{J}}\leq\min(1/e,4/(2\pi\underline{k}),e^{-1/(2\bar{k})})
. By Lemma
F.6
this holds because
\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{J}}\leq\frac{1}{2\pi e\sqrt{J}}
and I can trivially decrease
\underline{k}
and increase
\bar{k}
until the conditions hold.

Then since
V_{J,i}(\chi)
is the only random expression in
\overline{U}_{2k}
,

\displaystyle Pr\left(\overline{U}_{2k}\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}\log J+\frac{M_{J}^{2}(\log J)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{J}}\Delta_{J}\right)\leq\frac{2}{J},

using that
\Delta_{J}\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J}}
so
\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{C}}}{\Delta_{J}}\right)}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\log J}
.

Bounding
U_{3\alpha},U_{3\Omega}
:

I will follow the proof of Lemma SM6.4 in
Chen (2026)
to show that for
k\in\{\alpha,\Omega\}
,

\displaystyle Pr\left(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J},|U_{3k}|\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}\left\{e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}+\frac{M_{J}^{2}}{\sqrt{J}}\left(1+\sqrt{\log J}\right)\right\}\right)\leq\frac{2}{J}.

On the event
\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J}
,

\displaystyle U_{3k}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\{D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\right\}^{T}\Delta_{k,j}}_{V_{J}(\widehat{\chi})}+\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)^{T}\Delta_{k,j}.

Because
E_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}[D_{k,j}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)]=0
, it follows that

\displaystyle\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)=-E[D_{k,j}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})].

Then by Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle\|\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})^{1/2}\left(E[\|D_{k,j}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}^{2}]\right)^{1/2},

and since

\displaystyle\|D_{k,j}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}T_{k,j}(Z_{j})

where
T_{\alpha,j}(z)=\frac{\|\nabla f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)\|_{2}}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)}
and
T_{\Omega,j}(z)=\frac{\|Q_{j}(z,F_{0},\Psi_{j})\|_{2}}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}Pr(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M_{J})^{1/2}\left(E[T_{k,j}^{2}(Z_{j})]\right)^{1/2}.

Because both
T_{k,j}
are of the form
\|E[f(\tau,Z)|Z]\|_{2}
, by Jensen’s inequality
E[T_{k}^{2}]\leq E[\|f(\tau,Z_{j})\|_{2}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
. Then because
Z_{j}
is sub-Gaussian,

\displaystyle\|\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}.

Because of the truncation to
\|z\|_{2}\leq M_{J}
,

\displaystyle\|D_{k,j,M_{J}}(Z_{j},F_{0},\chi_{0},0)-\overline{D}_{k,j,M_{J}}(F_{0},\chi_{0},0)\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}

so for fixed
\chi,\chi_{1},\chi_{2}\in S
as defined in (
F.15
),

\displaystyle\left\|V_{J}(\chi_{1})-V_{J}(\chi_{2})\right\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}^{2}}{\sqrt{J}}\|\chi_{1}-\chi_{2}\|_{J},

\displaystyle\|V_{J}(\chi)\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{\Delta_{J}M_{J}^{2}}{\sqrt{J}}.

Then by the same chaining argument as for bounding
U_{2k}
but taking
u=\sqrt{\log J}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J}(\chi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}^{2}}{\sqrt{J}}\left[\left(1+\sqrt{\log J}\right)\Delta_{J}+\Delta_{J}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{C_{\mathcal{C}}}{\Delta_{J}}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{M_{J}^{2}}{\sqrt{J}}\left(1+\sqrt{\log J}\right)\Delta_{J}

with probability at least
1-2/J
, because
\Delta_{J}\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{J}}
. Thus letting

\displaystyle\overline{U}_{3k}=C_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\sup_{\chi\in S}|V_{J}(\chi)|+\Delta_{J}e^{-C_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}}\right),

the tail bound for
\overline{U}_{3k}
gives the result.

Bounding
R_{1},R_{2}
:

I follow the proofs of Lemmas SM6.5 and SM6.6 in
Chen (2026)
.

To bound
R_{1}
, it can be shown that each
R_{1j}
can be upper bounded by a constant times

\displaystyle\max\bigg(

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{\alpha,j}\|_{\infty}^{2}\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F},\|\Delta_{\alpha,j}\|_{\infty}\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\|_{op}\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F},

\displaystyle\qquad\|B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2}\|_{op}^{2}\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}\bigg)

By assumption and Lemma
F.17
, it follows that
|R_{1j}|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}^{2}M_{J}^{2}\log J\Rightarrow|R_{1}|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}^{2}M_{J}^{2}\log J
.

To bound
R_{2}
, I will show that

\displaystyle Pr\left(\|\widehat{\chi}-\chi_{0}\|_{J}\leq\Delta_{J},\overline{Z}_{J}\leq M_{J},|R_{2}|\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}^{2}\right)\leq\frac{1}{J}.

By the same logic as above,
\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})|R_{2}|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}^{2}\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})D_{j}
, where

\displaystyle D_{j}

\displaystyle\equiv\max\bigg(\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{F_{0},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F},\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{F_{0},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F},\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{F_{0},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}\bigg)

By the derivative calculations in Section
F.5.1
, these derivatives are functions of posterior moments under
F_{0}
, evaluated at transformed observation
\widehat{Z}_{j}(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega})
. Then Lemma
F.18
implies that on
A_{J}
,

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})D_{j}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})\max(\|\widehat{Z}_{j}(\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega})\|_{2},1)^{4}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\mathbbm{1}(A_{J})\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)^{4}.

By Chebyshev’s inequality, there exists some
C_{\mathcal{H}}
such that

\displaystyle Pr\left(\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)^{4}\geq C_{\mathcal{H}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{J}

because
Var(\frac{1}{J}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\max(\|Z_{j}\|_{2},1)^{4})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{J}
. Thus
Pr\left(A_{J},|R_{2}|\gtrsim_{\mathcal{H}}\Delta_{J}^{2}\right)\leq\frac{1}{J}
.

To conclude the proof of the theorem, I apply a union bound (as in Lemma SM6.13 in
Chen (2026)
) to the above rates to obtain the result, following Appendix SM6 of
Chen (2026)
. In the rate
\epsilon_{J}
, the first term comes from
U_{1\Omega}
, the second and fourth terms from
U_{2k}
, and the third term from
R_{1}
. The other rates derived are dominated. The leading terms in
\epsilon_{J}
dominate
\kappa_{J}
.
∎

F.5.3
Proof of Corollary
F.8

I first state a result, which is a multivariate analogue of Theorem SM7.1 in
Chen (2026)
, that will be used in the proof of the corollary.

Theorem F.13
.

Suppose
J\geq 7
.
Let
\tau_{j}|\Psi_{1:J}\sim F_{0}
, where
F_{0}
satisfies Assumption
1
.
Fix positive sequences
\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J}\to 0
with
\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J}\leq 1
, constants
\epsilon,C^{*}>0
. Consider the set of distributions that approximately maximize the likelihood

\displaystyle A(\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J})

\displaystyle=\{H\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}):\text{Sub}_{J}(H)\leq C^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\bar{h}(f_{H,\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\lambda_{J})\}

and consider the set of distributions that are far from
F_{0}
in
\bar{h}

\displaystyle B(t,\lambda_{J},\epsilon)

\displaystyle=\{H\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}):\bar{h}(f_{H,\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\geq tB\lambda_{J}^{1-\epsilon}\}

for some constant
B
to be chosen. Assume that for some
C_{\lambda}
,

\displaystyle\lambda_{J}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\gamma_{J}^{2}\geq\frac{C_{\lambda}}{J}(\log J)^{3}.

Then the probability that
A\cap B
is nonempty is bounded for
t>1
, that is, there exists a choice of
B
that depends on
\mathcal{H},C^{*},
and
C_{\lambda}
such that

\displaystyle Pr\left(A(\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J})\cap B(t,\lambda_{J},\epsilon)\neq\emptyset\right)

\displaystyle\leq(\log_{2}(1/\epsilon)+1)J^{-t^{2}}.

Proof.

The proof closely follows the proof of Theorem SM7.1 in
Chen (2026)
.
Decompose
B(t,\lambda_{J},\epsilon)\subseteq\cup_{k=1}^{K}B_{k}(t,\lambda_{J})
where for some
B>1
to be chosen and
K=\lceil|\log_{2}(1/\epsilon)|\rceil
,

\displaystyle B_{k}

\displaystyle=\left\{H:\bar{h}(f_{H,\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\in\left(tB\lambda_{J}^{1-2^{-k}},tB\lambda_{J}^{1-2^{-k+1}}\right]\right\}.

If
Pr(A(\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J})\cap B_{k}(t,\lambda_{J})\neq\emptyset)\leq J^{-t^{2}}
the result follows from a union bound.

Let
\mu_{J,k}=B\lambda_{J}^{1-2^{-k+1}}
, so that
B_{k}=\left\{H:\bar{h}(f_{H,\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\in\left(t\mu_{J,k+1},t\mu_{J,k}\right]\right\}
. Fix a
k\in[K]
.

For
\omega=\frac{1}{J^{2}}
consider an
\omega
-net for
\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
under
\|\cdot\|_{\infty,M}
(recall the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{\infty,M}
from (
F.19
)). Letting
N=N(\omega,\mathbb{F},\|\cdot\|_{\infty,M})
for
\mathbb{F}
the space of
f_{F,\cdot}
induced by
F\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
, let
H_{1},\dots,H_{N}
denote the distributions making up the
\omega
-net. And for each
i\in[N]
let
H_{k,i}
be a distribution, if it exists, with
\|f_{H_{k,i}}-f_{H_{i}}\|_{\infty,M}\leq\omega
and
\bar{h}(f_{H_{k,i},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\geq t\mu_{J,k+1}
. Finally let
I_{k}
collect the indices
i
for which
H_{k,i}
exists.

For any fixed distribution
H\in B_{k}(t,\lambda_{J})
there exists some
H_{i}
in the covering such that
\|f_{H}-f_{H_{i}}\|_{\infty,M}\leq\omega
. Furthermore
H
serves as witness that
H_{k,i}
exists with
\|f_{H}-f_{H_{k,i}}\|_{\infty,M}\leq 2\omega
.

Note that an upper bound for
f_{H,\Psi_{j}}(z)
is given by

\displaystyle f_{H,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(z)+2\omega&\|z\|_{2}\leq M\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}}&\|z\|_{2}>M.\end{cases}

Defining
v(z)=\omega\mathbbm{1}(\|z\|_{2}\leq M)+\omega\left(\frac{M}{\|z\|_{2}}\right)^{3}\mathbbm{1}(\|z\|_{2}>M)
,

\displaystyle f_{H,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(z)+2v(z)&\|z\|_{2}\leq M\\
\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(z)+2v(z)}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}2v(z)}&\|z\|_{2}>M.\end{cases}

This means the likelihood ratio between
F_{0}
and
H
is upper bounded:

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{J}\frac{f_{H,\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\max_{i\in I_{k}}\prod_{j=1}^{J}\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\right)\prod_{j:\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}2v(Z_{j})}.

If
H\in A(\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J})
the likelihood ratio is also lower bounded as in the proof of Theorem SM7.1 in
Chen (2026)
:

\displaystyle\prod_{j=1}^{J}\frac{f_{H,\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}

\displaystyle\geq\exp\left(-JC^{*}(t^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+t^{2}\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})\right),

so it follows that, choosing some
a>1
,

\displaystyle Pr\left(A(\gamma_{J},\lambda_{J})\cap B_{k}(t,\lambda_{J})\neq\emptyset\right)

\displaystyle\leq Pr\left(\left(\max_{i\in I_{k}}\prod_{j=1}^{J}\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\right)\prod_{j:\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}2v(Z_{j})}\geq\exp\left(-JC^{*}(t^{2}\lambda_{J}^{2}+t^{2}\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq Pr\left(\max_{i\in I_{k}}\prod_{j=1}^{J}\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}\geq e^{-Jt^{2}aC^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})}\right)

(F.16)

\displaystyle\hskip 18.49988pt+Pr\left(\prod_{j:\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}2v(Z_{j})}\geq e^{Jt^{2}(a-1)C^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})}\right).

(F.17)

By union bound, Markov’s inequality, and independence over
j
,

(
F.16
)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{k}}e^{Jt^{2}aC^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})/2}\prod_{j=1}^{J}E\left[\sqrt{\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}}\right].

Note

\displaystyle E\left[\sqrt{\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}}\right]

\displaystyle=\int\sqrt{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(z)+2v(z)}\sqrt{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)}dz

\displaystyle\leq 1-h^{2}(f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}},f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}})+\int\sqrt{2v(z)f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(z)}dz

\displaystyle\leq 1-h^{2}(f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}},f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}})+\sqrt{2\int v(z)dz}\qquad\text{Jensen's}

\displaystyle=1-h^{2}(f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}},f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}})+M\sqrt{6\pi\omega}

\displaystyle\Rightarrow\prod_{j=1}^{J}E\left[\sqrt{\frac{f_{H_{k,i},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})+2v(Z_{j})}{f_{F_{0},\Psi_{j}}(Z_{j})}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-J\bar{h}^{2}(f_{H_{k,i},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})+JM\sqrt{6\pi\omega}\right)

using
\prod_{j=1}^{J}t_{j}\leq\exp(\sum_{j=1}^{J}(t_{j}-1))
for
t_{j}>0
.
Then

(
F.16
)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in I_{k}}\exp\left(\frac{Jt^{2}aC^{*}}{2}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})-J\bar{h}^{2}(f_{H_{k,i},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})+JM\sqrt{6\pi\omega}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\frac{Jt^{2}aC^{*}}{2}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})-Jt^{2}\mu_{J,k+1}^{2}+M\sqrt{6\pi}+C(\log J)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log J}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log J}\right)\right)

because
\bar{h}(f_{H_{k,i},\cdot},f_{F_{0},\cdot})\geq t\mu_{J,k+1},|I_{k}|\leq N
,
\omega=\frac{1}{J^{2}}
, and
\log N\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log(1/\omega))^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\omega)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\omega)}\right)
by Suppl. Lemma 5 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
and Suppl. Lemma F.6 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
.

By Markov’s inequality, taking
x\mapsto x^{1/(2\log J)}
,

(
F.17
)

\displaystyle\leq E\left[\prod_{j=1}^{J}\left(\frac{1}{(\det(2\pi\Psi_{j}))^{1/6}}\frac{\|Z_{j}\|_{2}}{(2\omega)^{1/3}M}\right)^{\frac{3}{2\log J}\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M)}\right]\exp\left(-\frac{J(a-1)t^{2}C^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})}{2\log J}\right).

Define

\displaystyle a_{j}=\frac{1}{(\det(2\pi\Psi_{j}))^{1/6}(2\omega)^{1/3}M}\leq\frac{C_{\underline{k}}J^{2/3}}{M},\qquad\lambda=\frac{3}{2\log J}

for some constant
C_{\underline{k}}
depending only on
\underline{k},

then as in the proof of Theorem 7 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, using Suppl. Lemma 2 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
,

\displaystyle E\left[\prod_{j=1}^{J}\left(\frac{1}{(\det(2\pi\Psi_{j}))^{1/6}}\frac{\|Z_{j}\|_{2}}{(2\omega)^{1/3}M}\right)^{\frac{3}{2\log J}\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M)}\right]

\displaystyle=E\left[\left\{\prod_{j}(a_{j}\|Z_{j}\|_{2})^{\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M)}\right\}^{\lambda}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}a_{j}^{\lambda}E\left[\|Z_{j}\|_{2}^{\lambda}\mathbbm{1}(\|Z_{j}\|_{2}>M)\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(\sum_{j=1}^{J}a_{j}^{\lambda}M^{\lambda}\left(Ce^{-M^{2}/(8\bar{k})}+\left(\frac{2\mu_{q}}{M}\right)^{q}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(C_{\underline{k}}e\left(C+\frac{J2^{q}\mu_{q}^{q}}{M^{q}}\right)\right)

for some constant
C
, taking
M\geq\sqrt{8\bar{k}\log J}
and
\frac{3}{2\min(1,q)}\leq\log J
so that
\lambda\in(0,\min(1,q))
and defining
\mu_{q}\equiv(E[\|\tau\|_{2}^{q}])^{1/q}
for
\tau\sim F_{0}
.
Thus

(
F.17
)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(C_{\underline{k}}Ce+C_{\underline{k}}e\frac{J2^{q}\mu_{q}^{q}}{M^{q}}-\frac{J(a-1)t^{2}C^{*}(\gamma_{J}^{2}+\mu_{J,k}\lambda_{J})}{2\log J}\right).

Note that under Assumption
1
,
\mu_{q}\leq S_{0}\sqrt{q}
for all
q
, where
S_{0}\geq 1
without loss of generality.
So taking
a=2,q=c_{m}\log J,M=2eS_{0}\sqrt{c_{m}\log J}
where constant
c_{m}\geq 2
is chosen sufficiently large that
M\geq\sqrt{8\overline{k}\log J}
, then
\left(\frac{2\mu_{q}}{M}\right)^{q}\leq e^{-q}=J^{-c_{m}}
, so
J(2\mu_{q}/M)^{q}\leq 1/J
. Using
\lambda_{J}^{2}\geq\gamma_{J}^{2}\geq\frac{C_{\lambda}}{J}(\log J)^{3}
,

(
F.17
)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(C_{\underline{k}}Ce+\frac{C_{\underline{k}}e}{J}-t^{2}\frac{C^{*}C_{\lambda}(1+B)}{2}(\log J)^{2}\right)

(
F.16
)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\left(-t^{2}(\log J)^{3}\left(C_{\lambda}\left(-C^{*}-C^{*}B+B^{2}\right)-(C+5)4e^{2}S_{0}^{2}c_{m}\right)\right).

There exists large enough
B
such that
\eqref{eq:sm7.4}\leq 0.5\exp(-t^{2}\log J)
and
\eqref{eq:sm7.5}\leq 0.5\exp(-t^{2}\log J)
, so
\eqref{eq:sm7.4}+\eqref{eq:sm7.5}\leq J^{-t^{2}}
, which concludes the proof.
∎

Finally, the proof of Corollary
F.8
follows as in Appendix SM7 of
Chen (2026)
, which uses Corollary
F.12
and Theorem
F.13
, but replacing the constants
\alpha,\beta,
and
-p/(2p+1)
with
2,\frac{1}{2},
and
-\frac{1}{2}
respectively to match the rates
\Delta_{J},M_{J},\delta_{J}
chosen here.

F.6
Auxiliary lemmas

Lemma F.14
.

Fix a probability measure
F
on
\mathbb{R}^{2}
and any
z\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right)^{2}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f^{2}_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f_{F,\Psi_{j}}^{2}(z)}\right).

Also for every
z\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and all
\rho\in(0,1/2\pi\sqrt{e})
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{1/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho)

while for every
z\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
and all
\rho\in(0,1/2\pi e)
,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right)^{2}\frac{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\frac{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho).

Proof.

This lemma extends Suppl. Lemma F.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
to a heteroscedastic setting, using the approach of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
.

As in section D.2 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, for any fixed
j
let
F_{j}
denote the distribution of
\xi_{j}=\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\tau_{j}
where
\tau_{j}\sim F
. Then for
\check{z}_{j}=\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}z
one can verify

\displaystyle f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})

\displaystyle\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\nabla f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\nabla^{2}f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}.

Then using (F.1) in Suppl. Lemma F.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\nabla f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})\right\|_{2}}{f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})}\right)^{2}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f^{2}_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right).

By inspection of the proof, I can replace the trace with a Frobenius norm in equation (F.1) in Suppl. Lemma F.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
to obtain

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\Psi_{j}^{1/2}\left(I_{2}+\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})}{f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})}\right)\Psi_{j}^{1/2}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f_{F,\Psi_{j}}^{2}(z)}\right).

Similarly, from (F.2) in Suppl. Lemma F.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\nabla f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j}),\rho\right)}\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{1/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho)

and from (F.3) in Suppl. Lemma F.1 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\left\|\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\right)^{2}\frac{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1/2}\nabla f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})\right\|_{2}}{f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})}\right)^{2}\frac{f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j})}{\max\left(f_{F_{j},I_{2}}(\check{z}_{j}),\rho\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho).

Finally I follow the proof of Lemma SM6.8 in
Chen (2026)
and look at cases:

1)
f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\leq\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}
. Then because
t\log(1/(2\pi t)^{2})
is increasing over
t\in(0,1/2\pi e)
, using the result from above

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f_{F,\Psi_{j}}^{2}(z)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\rho\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi\rho)^{2}}\right)=\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\rho\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho).

The result follows from dividing by
\max\left(\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\rho\right)=\rho
.

2)
f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)>\frac{\rho}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}
. Then because
\log(1/(2\pi t)^{2})
is decreasing in
t
, using the result from above

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{2}\det(\Psi_{j})f_{F,\Psi_{j}}^{2}(z)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\log\left(\frac{1}{(2\pi\rho)^{2}}\right)=\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho).

∎

Lemma F.15
.

Let
f
be a density for random vector
Z\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Then for any
M,t>0
,

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbbm{1}(f(z)\leq t)f(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq(2M)^{n}t+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}Var(Z_{i})}{M^{2}}.

In particular, for
n=2
, choosing
M=t^{-1/4}\left(Var(Z_{1})+Var(Z_{2})\right)^{1/4}
gives

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathbbm{1}(f(z)\leq t)f(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq 5t^{1/2}\left(Var(Z_{1})+Var(Z_{2})\right)^{1/2}.

Proof.

As in the proof in
Chen (2026)
, assume without loss of generality that
E_{f}[Z]=0
.

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbbm{1}(f(z)\leq t)f(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbbm{1}(f(z)\leq t,\|z\|_{2}<M)f(z)dz+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\mathbbm{1}(f(z)\leq t,\|z\|_{2}\geq M)f(z)dz

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\|z\|_{2}<M}tdz+Pr(\|Z\|_{2}>M)

\displaystyle\leq(2M)^{n}t+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}Var(Z_{i})}{M^{2}}\qquad\text{multivariate Chebyshev}.

∎

Lemma F.16
.

Recall that
Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})=\int\varphi_{\Psi_{j}}(z-\tau)\text{vec}((z-\tau)\tau^{T})dF(\tau)
. For any
F,z,
and
\rho_{J}\in(0,e^{-1}/2\pi)
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\left(\|z\|_{2}\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{1/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})+\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho_{J})\right).

Then under the choices of
\rho_{J}
and
M_{J}
in Lemma
F.6
and Assumption
F.10
respectively, uniformly over
F,j\in[J]
, and
\|z\|_{2}\leq M_{J}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}.

Proof.

Note

\displaystyle\left\|Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\left\|E_{F,\Psi_{j}}[(z-\tau)|z]\right\|_{2}\left\|z\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\left\|E_{F,\Psi_{j}}[(z-\tau)(z-\tau)^{T}|z]\right\|_{F}.

Then from Lemma
F.14
,

\displaystyle\frac{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\left\|E_{F,\Psi_{j}}[(z-\tau)|z]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{1/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})

and

\displaystyle\frac{f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}\left\|E_{F,\Psi_{j}}[(z-\tau)(z-\tau)^{T}|z]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho_{J}).

Thus

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})\right\|_{2}}{\max\left(f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z),\frac{\rho_{J}}{\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})}}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{F}\left(\|z\|_{2}\left\|\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right\|_{F}^{1/2}\varphi_{+}(\rho_{J})+\varphi_{+}^{2}(\rho_{J})\right).

∎

Lemma F.17
.

Under the assumptions in Lemma
F.6
and Assumption
9
, suppose
(a_{j}(\tilde{\alpha}),B_{j}(\tilde{\Omega})^{1/2})
are on the line segment between
(a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha}),B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2})
and
(a_{j}(\alpha_{0}),B_{j}(\Omega_{0})^{1/2})
, and define
\tilde{\Psi}_{j},\tilde{Z}_{j}
accordingly. Then, second derivatives evaluated at
\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega},\tilde{Z}_{j}
satisfy

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\log J

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}\log J.

Proof.

Note that as in the proof of Lemma
F.6
,

\widehat{Z}_{j}=B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{-1/2}(B_{j}(\tilde{\Omega})^{1/2}\tilde{Z}_{j}+a_{j}(\tilde{\alpha})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha}))
, where
\|B_{j}(\tilde{\Omega})^{1/2}-B_{j}(\widehat{\Omega})^{1/2}\|_{\infty}\leq\Delta_{J}
and
\|a_{j}(\tilde{\alpha})-a_{j}(\widehat{\alpha})\|_{\infty}\leq\Delta_{J}
. Thus
\|\tilde{Z}_{j}\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}
. Furthermore by the same argument as in Lemma
F.6
,
f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})\sqrt{\det(\tilde{\Psi}_{j})}\geq\frac{1}{J^{4}}e^{-C_{H}\Delta_{J}M_{J}^{2}}
. Thus as in
Chen (2026)
,
|\log(f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})\sqrt{\det(\tilde{\Psi}_{j})})|\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J
.

Using Lemma
F.14
and properties of logarithms

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{j}\frac{\nabla f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})}\right)}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\log J}

and

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})^{T}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|\tilde{\Psi}_{j}+\tilde{\Psi}_{j}\frac{\nabla^{2}f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}}\tilde{\Psi}_{j}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log\left(\frac{1}{f_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}(\tilde{Z}_{j})}\right)\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J.

And note that because
\|\tilde{Z}_{j}\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}
and
\|\tau\|_{2}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}
under the support of
\widehat{F}_{J}
,
