\displaystyle\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)|\tilde{Z}_{j}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})(\tau_{j}-Z_{j})^{T}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[\tau_{j}-Z_{j}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{2}\left\|\tilde{Z}_{j}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J+M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\sqrt{\log J}

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}\left[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}|\tilde{Z}_{j}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}\left[(\tau_{j}\otimes I_{2})(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}|\tilde{Z}_{j}\right]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[\|\tau\|_{2}\|(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})(Z_{j}-\tau)^{T}\|_{F}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\log J.

Similarly, one can check

\displaystyle\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)\text{vec}\left((Z_{j}-\tau_{j})\tau_{j}^{T}\right)^{T}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\left\|E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[(\tau_{j}\otimes I_{2})(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})^{T}(\tau_{j}^{T}\otimes I_{2})|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq E_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\Psi}_{j}}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{2}\|(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})(Z_{j}-\tau_{j})^{T}\|_{F}|\tilde{Z}_{j}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}\log J.

Then plugging the above results into the derivative expressions derived in Section
F.5.1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial a_{j}^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log J

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial a_{j}\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}\log J

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}\psi_{j}}{\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})\partial\text{vec}(B_{j}^{1/2})^{T}}\bigg|_{\widehat{F}_{J},\tilde{\alpha},\tilde{\Omega}}\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M_{J}^{2}\log J.

where the final line follows because the derivative is a sum of the above derived terms times functions of
\Omega
and
\Sigma
.
∎

Lemma F.18
.

Suppose
\tau\sim F_{0}
,
Z|\tau\sim N_{2}(\tau,\Psi)
, and
\underline{\psi}I_{2}\preceq\Psi\preceq\overline{\psi}I_{2}
. Suppose also that Assumptions
1
and
8
hold. Then for every
p>0
,
E_{F_{0},\Psi}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}|Z=z]\lesssim_{p,\mathcal{H}}\left(\max\{\|z\|_{2},1\}\right)^{p}
.

Proof.

Define
\|x\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\equiv x^{T}\Psi^{-1}x
and let
M=\max\{\|z\|_{\Psi^{-1}},M_{0}\}
, where
M_{0}
is a sufficiently large constant such that, uniformly over
\Psi
,
F_{0}(\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq M_{0})\geq\frac{3}{4}
.
M_{0}
exists by Markov’s inequality, noting
E[\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}]\leq\underline{\psi}^{-1}E[\|\tau\|_{2}^{2}]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}1
.

I can write

\displaystyle\int\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\right)dF_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq\left(3\sqrt{\overline{\psi}}M\right)^{p}\int\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\right)dF_{0}(\tau)+e^{-2M^{2}}\int_{\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}>3M}\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}dF_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq\left(3\sqrt{\overline{\psi}}M\right)^{p}\int\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\right)dF_{0}(\tau)+e^{-2M^{2}}E_{F_{0}}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}],

using that
\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}>3M
implies
\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\geq\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}-\|z\|_{\Psi^{-1}}>2M
.

Furthermore, since
\|z\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq M
,

\displaystyle\int\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\right)dF_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle\geq\int_{\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq M}\exp\left(-\frac{1}{2}\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}^{2}\right)dF_{0}(\tau)

\displaystyle\geq e^{-2M^{2}}F_{0}(\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq M)\geq\frac{3}{4}e^{-2M^{2}},

using that
\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq M
implies
\|z-\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq\|z\|_{\Psi^{-1}}+\|\tau\|_{\Psi^{-1}}\leq 2M
.
Thus

\displaystyle E_{F_{0},\Psi}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}|Z=z]

\displaystyle\leq\left(3\sqrt{\overline{\psi}}M\right)^{p}+\frac{4}{3}E_{F_{0}}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}].

Assumption
1
implies
E_{F_{0}}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}]\lesssim_{p,\mathcal{H}}1
. Furthermore,
M\leq\max\{\underline{\psi}^{-1/2}\|z\|_{2},M_{0}\}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\max\{\|z\|_{2},1\}
. Thus

\displaystyle E_{F_{0},\Psi}[\|\tau\|_{2}^{p}|Z=z]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p,\mathcal{H}}\left(\max\{\|z\|_{2},1\}\right)^{p}.

∎

Lemma F.19
.

Recalling
d_{\alpha,\infty,M}
and
d_{\Omega,\infty,M}
from (
F.14
), the following bounds hold for
\eta\leq\min\left(1/e,4/(2\pi\underline{k}),e^{-1/(2\bar{k})}\right)
under the assumptions of Theorem
F.11
:

\displaystyle\log N\left(\frac{1+\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}\eta,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\alpha,\infty,M}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log(1/\eta))^{3}\max\left\{1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right\}

\displaystyle\log N\left(\frac{1+M\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}+\log(1/\rho_{J})}{\rho_{J}}\eta,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\Omega,\infty,M}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log(1/\eta))^{3}\max\left\{1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right\}.

Proof.

Fix some
\|z\|_{2}\leq M
. Let
T_{\alpha,j}=\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)
and
T_{\Omega,j}=Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})
.
As in the proof of Proposition SM6.2 in
Chen (2026)
,

\displaystyle\|D_{k,j}(z,F_{1},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-D_{k,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\rho_{J}}\|T_{k,j}(z,F_{1},\chi_{0})-T_{k,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\|T_{k,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0})\|_{2}}{\rho_{J}\max(f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z),\rho_{J}/\sqrt{\det(\Psi_{j})})}|f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)|.

Then by Lemmas
F.14
and
F.16
,

\displaystyle\|D_{\alpha,j}(z,F_{1},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-D_{\alpha,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\rho_{J}}\|\nabla f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-\nabla f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}|f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)|

\displaystyle\|D_{\Omega,j}(z,F_{1},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})-D_{\Omega,j}(z,F_{2},\chi_{0},\rho_{J})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\rho_{J}}\|Q_{j}(z,F_{1},\Psi_{j})-Q_{j}(z,F_{2},\Psi_{j})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{M\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}+\log(1/\rho_{J})}{\rho_{J}}|f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)|.

Define

\displaystyle K_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\equiv\int(z-\tau)(z-\tau)^{T}\varphi_{\Psi_{j}}(z-\tau)dF(\tau).

(F.18)

Note that I can write

\displaystyle Q_{j}(z,F,\Psi_{j})

\displaystyle=\text{vec}\left(-\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\Psi_{j}\nabla f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)z^{\prime}-K_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right).

Then it follows that

\displaystyle\|Q_{j}(z,F_{1},\Psi_{j})-Q_{j}(z,F_{2},\Psi_{j})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\left\|\Psi_{j}\right\|_{op}\left\|z\right\|_{2}\left\|\nabla f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-\nabla f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|K_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-K_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}M\left\|\nabla f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-\nabla f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{2}+\left\|K_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-K_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{F}.

Similar to Appendix C of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
define

\displaystyle\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{\|z\|_{2}\leq M}|f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)|

(F.19)

\displaystyle\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\nabla,M}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{\|z\|_{2}\leq M}\|\nabla f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-\nabla f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\|_{2}

(F.20)

\displaystyle\|K_{F_{1},\cdot}-K_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\nabla^{2},M}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{\|z\|_{2}\leq M}\|K_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-K_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\|_{F}.

(F.21)

Then

\displaystyle d_{\alpha,\infty,M}(F_{1},F_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\rho_{J}}\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\nabla,M}+\frac{\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle d_{\Omega,\infty,M}(F_{1},F_{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\frac{1}{\rho_{J}}\|K_{F_{1},\cdot}-K_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\nabla^{2},M}+\frac{M}{\rho_{J}}\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\nabla,M}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{M\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}+\log(1/\rho_{J})}{\rho_{J}}\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\|_{\infty,M}

Let
\mathbb{F}
be the space of functions
f_{F,\cdot}
induced by the space of distributions
F\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
.
By Suppl. Lemma 5 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, Suppl. Lemma 6 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, and Suppl. Lemma F.6 of
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
, if
\eta\leq\min\left(1/e,4/(2\pi\underline{k})\right)

\displaystyle\log N(\eta,\mathbb{F},\|\cdot\|_{\infty,M})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right)

\displaystyle\log N(\eta,\mathbb{F},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla,M})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right).

Let
\mathbb{K}
be the space of functions
K_{F,\cdot}
induced by the space of distributions
F\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
. By Lemma
F.20
if
\eta\leq e^{-1/(2\bar{k})}

\displaystyle\log N(\eta,\mathbb{K},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log 1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right).

Thus

\displaystyle\log N\left(\frac{1+\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}}{\rho_{J}}\eta,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\alpha,\infty,M}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right)

\displaystyle\log N\left(\frac{1+M\sqrt{\log(1/\rho_{J})}+\log(1/\rho_{J})}{\rho_{J}}\eta,\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2}),d_{\Omega,\infty,M}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right).

∎

Lemma F.20
.

Recall
\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M}
from (
F.21
) and
\mathbb{K}
the space of
K_{F,\Psi_{j}}
induced by distributions
F\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
, for
K_{F,\Psi_{j}}
defined by (
F.18
).
Then for
\eta\leq e^{-1/(2\bar{k})}
, under the assumptions of Theorem
F.11
,
\log N(\eta,\mathbb{K},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log(1/\eta))^{3}\max\left\{1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right\}.

Proof.

The proof follows the proof of Suppl. Lemmas 3-6 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
. Define the second-derivative metric

\displaystyle\left\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}

\displaystyle\equiv\max_{j\in[J]}\sup_{\left\|z\right\|_{2}\leq M}\left\|\nabla^{2}f_{F_{1},\Psi_{j}}(z)-\nabla^{2}f_{F_{2},\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{F}.

Note that

\displaystyle K_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)=\sqrt{\det(2\pi\Psi_{j})}\left(\Psi_{j}\nabla^{2}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\Psi_{j}+\Psi_{j}f_{F,\Psi_{j}}(z)\right),

so there exists some constant
C_{\mathcal{H}}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|K_{F_{1},\cdot}-K_{F_{2},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2},M}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathcal{H}}\left(\left\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}+\left\|f_{F_{1},\cdot}-f_{F_{2},\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}\right).

Fix a distribution
F\in\mathcal{P}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
and fix some
a\geq 1
to be chosen. Define the sets
S_{M}\equiv\{z\in\mathbb{R}^{2}:\|z\|_{2}\leq M\}
and
S_{M}^{a}\equiv\{z:d(z,S_{M})\leq a\}
, and let
L=N(a,S_{M}^{a},\|\cdot\|_{2})
. Using the multivariate moment-matching construction of Suppl. Lemmas 3 and 4 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, with the degree of the matched moments increased by two, there exists a discrete distribution
H
supported on
S_{M}^{a}
with at most

\displaystyle l\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}a^{4}L+1

atoms such that

\displaystyle\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H,\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}e^{-a^{2}/(2\bar{k})}

and

\displaystyle\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H,\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla_{f}^{2},M}\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(1+a^{2})e^{-a^{2}/(2\bar{k})},

where the second bound follows because

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}\varphi_{\Psi_{j}}(z)=\left(\Psi_{j}^{-1}zz^{T}\Psi_{j}^{-1}-\Psi_{j}^{-1}\right)\varphi_{\Psi_{j}}(z)

so it is sufficient to match multivariate moments with two additional moments relative to Suppl. Lemma 3 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
, and uniformly over
j
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\|z\|_{2}\geq a}\left\|\nabla^{2}\varphi_{\Psi_{j}}(z)\right\|_{F}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(1+a^{2})e^{-a^{2}/(2\bar{k})}

so the same argument used in Suppl. Lemmas 3 and 4 of
Soloff
et al. (2025)
applies.

Let
\mathcal{C}
be a minimal
\alpha
-net of
S^{a}_{M}
and let
H^{\prime}
approximate each atom of
H
with its closest element from
\mathcal{C}
, so that
H=\sum_{i}w_{i}\delta_{a_{i}}
and
H^{\prime}=\sum_{i}w_{i}\delta_{b_{i}}
where
w_{i}
are convex weights. Since the first and third derivatives of the Gaussian density are uniformly bounded under eigenvalue bounds on
\Psi_{j}
, the mean value theorem gives

\displaystyle\left\|f_{H,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}+\left\|f_{H,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\alpha.

Let
\Delta_{l-1}
be the
(l-1)
-simplex of probability vectors in
l
dimensions and let
\mathcal{D}
be a minimal
\beta
-net of
\Delta_{l-1}
in the
\|\cdot\|_{1}
norm. Let
H^{\prime\prime}
be the distribution that approximates the weights
w
by their closest element
v\in\mathcal{D}
, so
H^{\prime\prime}=\sum_{i}v_{i}\delta_{b_{i}}
. Then because the Gaussian density and its Hessian are uniformly bounded,

\displaystyle\left\|f_{H^{\prime},\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}+\left\|f_{H^{\prime},\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\beta.

So combining the previous bounds,

\displaystyle\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}+\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(1+a^{2})e^{-a^{2}/(2\bar{k})}+\alpha+\beta.

For any
\eta\leq e^{-1/(2\bar{k})}
choose
\delta=c_{0}\eta^{2}
,
\alpha=\beta=\delta
and
a=\sqrt{2\bar{k}\log(1/\alpha)}
, where
c_{0}\in(0,1]
is a sufficiently small constant to be chosen later. Note that
a\geq 1
. Furthermore because
0<\eta<1
,

\displaystyle(1+a^{2})e^{-a^{2}/(2\bar{k})}+\alpha+\beta

\displaystyle=c_{0}\eta^{2}\left(3+2\bar{k}\log(1/c_{0}\eta^{2})\right)=c_{0}\eta^{2}\left(3+2\bar{k}\log(1/c_{0})+4\bar{k}\log(1/\eta)\right)

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\eta\left(3+2\bar{k}\log(1/c_{0})+\frac{4\bar{k}}{e}\right).

I can choose
c_{0}
sufficiently small that

\displaystyle\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2}_{f},M}+\left\|f_{F,\cdot}-f_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\infty,M}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathcal{H}}^{-1}\eta

for the constant
C_{\mathcal{H}}
defined above, so that

\displaystyle\left\|K_{F,\cdot}-K_{H^{\prime\prime},\cdot}\right\|_{\nabla^{2},M}

\displaystyle\leq\eta.

Following the argument of Suppl. Lemma 5 in
Soloff
et al. (2025)
and the proof of Theorem 4.1 in
Saha and
Guntuboyina (2020)
, the number of possible
H^{\prime\prime}
is bounded above, up to a constant that depends on
\mathcal{H}
, by
\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)^{l}\left(1+\frac{N(\alpha,S_{M}^{a},\|\cdot\|_{2})}{l}\right)^{l}\equiv A^{l}
.
Thus
\log N(\eta,\mathbb{K},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}l\log A
.

Note that
\log A\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log\left[\left(1+\frac{2}{\beta}\right)\left(1+\frac{a}{\alpha}\right)^{2}\right]\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\delta)
. Finally note that
L=N(a,S_{M}^{a},\|\cdot\|_{2})\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}\left(1+\frac{M}{a}\right)^{2}
. Together with the expressions for
l
and
L
,

\displaystyle\log N(\eta,\mathbb{K},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}a^{4}\left(1+\frac{M}{a}\right)^{2}\log(1/\delta)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log 1/\delta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\delta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\delta)}\right).

Since
\log(1/\delta)=2\log(1/\eta)+\log(1/c_{0})\asymp_{\mathcal{H}}\log(1/\eta)
,

\displaystyle\log N(\eta,\mathbb{K},\|\cdot\|_{\nabla^{2},M})

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathcal{H}}(\log 1/\eta)^{3}\max\left(1,\frac{M}{\sqrt{\log(1/\eta)}},\frac{M^{2}}{\log(1/\eta)}\right).

∎
